Gallery index

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

A_only is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged pipeline._report_text helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside <pre> blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

01. s1321_w00_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: baseline.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 03f9db39acb702a959efd7f9d2a73b8c949b420de4fadddb7bd15acaad066a45.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (copied prior origins noted in the packet) place blue at n0 peaked at cell [0,2] (peak probability ~0.9998) and red at n0 peaked at [0,0] (~0.9986). At n1 blue peaks at [1,0] (~0.99999) and red at [0,2] (~0.9908). Node n2 is uniform in both roles. Node n3 shows broader uncertainty: blue peaks at [1,1] (~0.577) and red at [1,2] (~0.416). Candidate conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) provide alternate conditional estimates but are not events that have occurred; copied/inherited entries are not new estimates. Across k0/k1/k2 the nodes n1, n2 and n3 match the inherited/current patterns; n0 shows modest variation in peak probabilities across commands (blue peak probability ranges from ~0.920 to ~0.9998; red remains peaked at [0,0] near ~0.99–0.999). Relations supplied describe relative peak positions but do not assert physical facts.

02. s1321_w00_t12_A_A_only_current_donor

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: current_donor.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 8c08ab4a8d6470226c4d6d8be1119aec25577b043f58662be48f19e8d8d8fb01.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current view (inherited/copied prior records) shows: n0 — blue peak at cell [0,2] (peak ≈0.9998), red peak at [0,0] (≈0.9986); n1 — blue peak at [1,0] (≈0.999987), red peak at [0,2] (≈0.9908); n2 — both roles uniform across all 16 cells (equal peaks); n3 — both roles peak at [1,2] with lower peak probabilities (~0.265 blue, ~0.275 red). Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 provide alternative conditional estimates (command_estimate origins) for the same panel and nodes and are not executed events; copied prior records are not new estimates. Differences across candidate records are mainly in n0: blue peak probability varies (k0 ~0.920, k1 ~0.992, k2 ~0.927) while red remains peaked at [0,0] with very high probability across candidates. Other nodes' candidate peaks match the current-view peak patterns described above.

03. s1321_w00_t12_A_A_only_current_restored

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: current_restored.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: baa1e95f5dee5744584ce0d53a6f1188921072e28761f8f68e41363f9a7082d6.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (copied prior records where noted) place blue at n0 peak (row 0, col 2) and red at n0 peak (0,0) with very high peak probabilities; n1 blue peaks at (1,0) and red at (0,2); n2 is uniform for both roles (all 16 cells tied); n3 blue peaks at (1,1) and red at (1,2). Candidate command records (k0, k1, k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Across candidates the n0 peaks are stable (blue at (0,2), red at (0,0)) but their peak probabilities differ by command (k0, k1, k2 show different peak probabilities); n1, n2 and n3 peak locations are consistent across candidate commands and match the current panel entries or inherited copies. Origins and represented times are recorded with each distribution; copied prior records are explicitly not new independent estimates.

04. s1321_w00_t12_A_A_only_prospective_donor

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: prospective_donor.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: b1d70194c31d76a0543d16dffbee186dbffd4cddc26cb822b2fb0daed9f632cf.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains probabilistic location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (copied or inherited from prior records at the represented times) are not new measurements; command-conditioned candidate estimates (k0,k1,k2) are conditional forecasts, not events that have already occurred. Current (panel p0) peaks: n0 blue at (0,2) and red at (0,0) with very high peak probabilities; n1 blue at (1,0) and red at (0,2) (high peaks); n2 is uniform for both roles (all 16 cells tied); n3 blue peaks at (1,1) and red at (1,2) with moderate peak probabilities. Candidate records differ primarily at node n0: blue peak probability varies across k0/k1/k2 (around 0.976–0.997) and red peak probability varies similarly (around 0.996–0.998); other nodes’ peak locations are consistent across the candidate commands and current view. No physical presence, sensor truth or confidence beyond the supplied distributional numbers is asserted.

Candidate (conditional) records for commands k0, k1, k2 provide alternative distributions: where candidates differ from current, peaks and peak probabilities change across commands (examples include n0 and n3 in both panels). Copied or inherited records are not new executed events; native conditional records are conditional estimates, not executed branches.

Narrator text:

This packet reports, per panel (p0, p1) and per node (n0–n3), fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles. Current (non‑conditional) estimates: p0 — n0 blue peaks at (0,2) and red at (0,0) (very high peak probabilities); n1 blue at (1,0) and red at (0,2); n2 is uniform for both roles; n3 blue at (1,1) and red at (1,2). p1 — n0 has four equally maximal blue cells ((0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)) and four equally maximal red cells ((0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)); n1 matches the narrow peaks at (1,0) (blue) and (0,2) (red); n2 uniform; n3 has four‑cell peaks for each role (blue: (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); red: (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)). Candidate conditional records (commands k0,k1,k2) present alternative distributions; where multiple commands are provided the modal cells are the same for many nodes (e.g. p0 n0 blue remains (0,2) across k0–k2 but with different peak probabilities). Copied or conditional origins are not events and are reported as supplied.

Narrator text:

Two panels (p0, p1) show fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (not new when inherited or copied): in p0, n0 blue peaks at (0,2) (prob ≈0.999814), red at (0,0) (≈0.998581); n1 blue peaks at (1,0) (≈0.999987), red at (0,2) (≈0.990755); n2 is uniform (all 16 cells equal); n3 blue peaks at (1,1) (≈0.57694), red at (1,2) (≈0.41609). In p1 current, n0 and n1 match p0, n2 is uniform, and n3’s blue mass is concentrated on the four top-left cells (each ≈0.22869) while red concentrates on the four top-right cells (each ≈0.13438). Candidate command records (k0, k1, k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events; copied/inherited entries are not new estimates. Across commands, peak locations are often consistent (n1,n2), while n0 and n3 show command-dependent changes in peak probabilities (e.g. n0 blue peak probability ranges ≈0.920–0.9998 across k0–k2).

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains current and three conditional command candidate distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (copied prior record, represented_time 12) peaks: n0 blue at (0,2), red at (0,0); n1 blue at (1,0), red at (0,2); n2 both uniform over the 4×4 grid; n3 both peak at (1,1). Candidate command estimates (k0,k1,k2; represented_time 13) match current at n1,n2,n3. At n0 the candidates also peak at the same cells but with lower blue peak probabilities (~0.85–0.87 for k0–k2) compared with the current blue peak (~0.9998); red peak probabilities at n0 are ~0.99–0.998 for candidates and ~0.9986 current. Copied prior records here are inherited, not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional estimates, not events that have already occurred.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions (represented time 12) show: n0 — blue peak at (0,2) with peak probability ≈0.9998 and red peak at (0,0) with ≈0.9986; n1 — blue peak (1,0) ≈0.999987 and red peak (0,2) ≈0.99075; n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells (each 0.0625); n3 — blue peak (1,0) ≈0.2421 and red peak (2,2) ≈0.2397. Origins labeled as copied prior records or inherited history mean those current entries are copied or inherited, not new estimates. Candidate conditional command records (k0, k1, k2) are conditional forecasts, not events that have already occurred. Across candidates the only substantive variation appears at n0: blue peaks at (0,2) and red at (0,0) for all commands but with differing peak probabilities (k0 ~0.856/0.988, k1 ~0.848/0.997, k2 ~0.871/0.999 for blue/red respectively). Nodes n1, n2 and n3 have the same peak cells across the provided conditional records.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 and p1 contain paired red/blue 4×4 location distributions at nodes n0–n3. Current records (represented_time 12–13) show, for both panels: n0 blue peaked at cell [0,2] and red at [0,0]; n1 blue peaked at [1,0] and red at [0,2]; n2 uniform (all 16 cells tied); n3 peaks differ by panel: p0 has both roles peaked at [1,1], p1 has blue peaked on the upper-left 2×2 block and red on the upper-right 2×2 block. Across command-conditioned candidates the modal cells are stable for n1 and n2; n0 and n3 show alternative conditional modes (examples: p1 n0 ranges from a four-cell block to a single cell to uniform across k0–k2).

Narrator text:

This packet contains two panels, p0 and p1, each giving fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Current entries are inherited or copied prior records (not new executed events); candidate entries (commands k0,k1,k2) are conditional command estimates (not executed). In panel p0 current: n0 peaks — blue at (0,2), red at (0,0); n1 — blue at (1,0), red at (0,2); n2 — both uniform across all 16 cells; n3 — both peak at (1,1). Conditional candidates in p0 largely mirror those peaks for n0, n1 and n3; n2 remains uniform in all candidates. In panel p1 current: n0’s blue mass concentrates on four top-right cells while red concentrates on four top-left cells; n1, n2, n3 follow the same peak patterns shown for p0/current or the candidates above. All numeric entries are probability distributions only; copied records and conditional command records are labeled as such and are not observations of events.

Narrator text:

This packet contains, for panels p0 and p1 at represented time 13, fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (non‑command) estimates: p0 n0 — blue peaked at [0,2] (peak ≈0.9998), red at [0,0] (≈0.9986); p0 n1 — blue peaked at [1,0] (≈0.99999), red at [0,2] (≈0.991); p0 n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells; p0 n3 — blue peaked at [1,1] (≈0.794), red peaked at [1,1] (≈0.343). Panel p1 current distributions match the p1 current peaks above. Conditional command records k0/k1/k2 are explicit hypothetical estimates (not executed events); copied priors are marked as inherited and are not new estimates. Differences across commands include node‑level shifts in peak probability and pattern: e.g. p0 n0 blue peak probability is lower under k0 (~0.856) and higher under k2 (~0.871), while red peak at [0,0] increases from k0 to k2 (~0.988 → ~0.999). Panel p1 k0 shows quadrant‑split peaks for both roles (multiple equal maxima) unlike the single‑cell peaks in other command conditions.

This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Copied prior records and inherited snapshots are not new estimates; native conditional records (commands k0, k1, k2) are conditional/counterfactual, not executed events. Current distributions (panel p0): n0 concentrates blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0]; n1 concentrates blue at [1,0] and red at [0,2]; n2 is uniform for both roles; n3 places blue on the upper-left 2×2 and red on the upper-right 2×2. Panel p1 current is similar for n1–n3 but differs at n0 (p1 current spreads blue over the two rightmost columns of the top two rows and spreads red over the two leftmost columns of the top two rows). Conditional candidates vary: k1 often concentrates probability onto single cells (e.g. n0 and n1), k0 and k2 can be broader or uniform in some nodes. The packet is data only.

42. s1321_w00_t12_analytic_joined_W_restored

Source: analytic; view: joined; variant: W_restored.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 1335cc7087d40424d3fbbec7c1c31f8070f6bac0100cbd0ad1741f4f9cc302a6.

Narrator text:

This packet contains two panels (p0, p1). Each panel gives fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at four addressed nodes (n0–n3). Current entries are inherited or copied prior/completed records and are not new estimates; conditional candidate records (commands k0, k1, k2) are conditional branches, not events that already occurred. Current summaries: in both panels the current distribution for n0 is sharply peaked for blue at cell [0,2] and for red at [0,0]; n1 current blue peaks at [1,0] and red at [0,2]; n2 current distributions are uniform across all 16 cells for both roles; n3 current blue peaks on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1 and red peaks on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 2–3. Conditional records differ: e.g. for n0 under k1 both roles are tightly concentrated (blue [0,2], red [0,0]), under k0 they spread over 2×2 blocks with lower peak probabilities, and under k2 some candidates return uniform distributions. All numeric peaks are listed in the propositions.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (record_time 12): n0 — blue peak at [0,2] (probability ~0.9998), red peak at [0,0] (~0.9986). n1 — blue peak at [1,0] (~0.99999), red peak at [0,2] (~0.9908). n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells (each 0.0625). n3 — blue peaks on the four-cell set {[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]} (peak prob ~0.2287), red peaks on {[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]} (peak prob ~0.1344). Candidate conditional records differ by command key: for example, k1 matches current for n0 but k0 gives a four-cell blue and red peak at n0 (less concentrated), and k2 sets n0 uniform. Some candidate entries are inherited copies of prior completed records (not new estimates); native conditional records are conditional forecasts, not events that have already occurred.
